(331.9 s wall; `luasum.json` 34120 bytes, sha256 `37d6857c…48f71c4`, byte-for-byte identical to the run copy; each log file byte-identical to `testing/runs/x223a-20261007-085224/server/Logs/` or `clients/bob/Logs/`, renamed only by the `server-`/`bob-` prefix). **Plan 10 Task S6, the Lua checksum arm, boot a**: fixture `two`, build 42.20.4, `Nutrition` false, `DoLuaChecksum` true and `AntiCheatChecksum` 2 in the server ini (`phases.boot.ini`); the whitelist reads `admin` role 7 and `bob` role 2, the role table naming 7 `admin` and 2 `user` (`db`); `doctor_clean` true, `lint_errors` empty, both verify rows ok, `server_error_count` 0, `mod_error_lines` empty, `admin_lua_error` false.

How to read it. `meta.frozen_file` and `meta.copy_file` hash the changed file before the boot, and `phases.boot.deployed_NR_Core` hashes the three copies the run deployed (server, `admin`, `bob`): the server's and `admin`'s carry the frozen sha256 `a4a60710…1e77f3d`, `bob`'s `f67e15e9…f7201ab4`, all 3272 bytes with 0 CRs, the CR-dropped MD5s `1283f170…` against `927032ae…`; `meta.tree_diff` finds 65 files on each side and one differing, `common/media/lua/shared/NR_Core.lua`. `live.server.hits` and `live.bob.hits` are both logs tailed during the watch with the driver's wall clock (0.25 s polls); `logs.*` are the same logs grepped after teardown with every harness echo line (`PZTK: `) dropped; `logs.bob_console_tail` is the last 120 lines of `bob`'s console. The three `.txt` files carry the game's own millisecond stamps and are the timing to cite.

- `bob` was launched at wall 79.621 (`phases.boot.bob_start_wall`). Its connection log runs `login` 08:54:01.657, `lua-connected` 08:54:07.523, `login-queue-request` 08:54:07.878, then `force-disconnect` at 08:54:10.064 with the message `checksum-File doesn't match the one on the server:` followed by `media/lua/shared/nr_core.lua` and the absolute path of `bob`'s own copy, and `disconnect` at 08:54:10.088.
- The server's user log: `"bob" attempting to join used queue` 08:54:07.882; `Anti-cheat="ChecksumUpdate" is triggered for connection="bob" server-option="AntiCheatChecksum" reason="Lua incorrect checksum" counter=1/1 action="Kick"` 08:54:08.837; `Connection disconnect index=1` 08:54:10.088. No `fully connected` line for `bob`. Its connections log has `bob` at `role="user"` and its `disconnect` `receive-disconnect` at 08:54:10.101.
- The server stdout (`logs.server_mismatch.hits`): `WARN : Multiplayer ... at ChecksumPacket.parseServer > user bob will be kicked in 60000ms because Lua/script checksums do not match`, then the `AntiCheat.log` line above; the server's own `luaChecksum: 6191c643de0fc74cb1503a69ff655be3` at boot. No `Timed out connection because checksum was different` line anywhere in the server log (`logs.server_mismatch`, the whole file). `bob`'s console (`logs.bob_console_tail`) ends with `client: DoLuaChecksum start` and no `DoLuaChecksum end` (`admin`'s console has both), then `LuaEventManager: adding unknown event "OnDisconnect"`.
- `bob` never reached ready (no `phases.boot.bob_ready_wall`; `client_seen` for `bob` holds no marker); its process stayed alive to the window's end at wall 259.7 (`phases.boot.bob_alive_at_end`), and the teardown's graceful quit timed out on it. `reads`: `players_at_end` `["admin"]`; `admin_tk_at_end` 1. `db.userlog` holds one row, `bob`, `LuaChecksum`, `Lua`, `ChecksumPacket`, `1`, `2026-10-07 08:54:08`. `admin`'s console received the anti-cheat line as an admin-chat message (`logs.admin_mismatch.hits`).

**`x224-20261007-092319/globals2.json`** — produced by `testing/experiments/x224_globals2.py`, written before the boot and committed at `caab8dd` with the probe `testing/experiments/TKX_GlobalsProbe2` and the profile `testing/profiles/x22-globals2.toml` before the run, unedited since, on HEAD `caab8dd` (harness Lua `fadf698`, clean; harness Python `aaa51f4`; `mod/` not booted; the frozen tree's digest `05da403f…fd452fb6` over 65 files in `meta.frozen_tree`)
(493.6 s wall; `globals2.json` 1300921 bytes, sha256 `596d0493…fa37d11`, byte-for-byte identical to the run copy). **Plan 10 Task S1b, the zeroed-rates route's unmeasured seats**: fixture `two`, `admin` -debug then `bob` release, Nutrition false, DayLength 4 (37 or 38 server ticks between two writes), `[sandbox.NR]` Mode 2 and Severity 1.5, SleepAllowed and SleepNeeded true; every verify row ok; `server_error_count` 0; `admin` not parked; the frozen copy's self-report read `mode=overlay … hook=false` (`logs.server.hook_lines`).

How to read it. The probe keeps string fields in the global `TKX_G2` (`phases.A`). Each game minute, on the server, it reads each online player's three stats (pre), writes the targets through `stats:set(CharacterStat.X, v)` and reads them back (post); a writer ring row per player per write carries `d = pre − that player's previous post` and the server tick (`phases.<P>.ring.rows`). While armed, an `OnTick` sampler keeps one row per server tick for `admin`: the tick, the minute counter, HUNGER, THIRST, FATIGUE, the armed canteen's litres and the `IsRunning`, `isPlayerMoving`, `isSprinting` and in-`SwipeStatePlayer` flags of the server's copy (`phases.<P>.samples.rows`). A sampler row whose `minute` is one more than the row before it is the first row after a write.

- **A** (server): `fileScopeNR` `NR:table;Mode=1;Severity=1` (the defaults); in `OnGameBoot` `bootNRMode` `2`, `bootNRSeverity` `1.5`, `bootSide` `server`, `bootGate` `zero (Mode 2)`; `bootTableBefore` all six rise keys at their shipped values, `bootSet` all six 0; `startedTable` all seven 0. The frozen copy read `options.mode` 2 and `fast.registered` false (`phases.S0.nr`). On `admin`'s client the `OnGameBoot` handler read Mode 1 and Severity 1, and `OnGameStart` Mode 2.
- **W** (`autodrink.set` true, a `Base.CanteenMilitaryFull` of 0.9 L, the THIRST target raised to 0.3): at the first write (tick 1616) the next sampled tick read THIRST 0.3 with 0.9 L, the one after THIRST 0 and 0.3 L (a 0.6 L drink); THIRST then read 0 at all 36 samples until the next write, whose pre read 0 (`d` −0.3); that write's 0.3 drank the remaining 0.3 L one tick later, leaving THIRST 0.15 at all 36 samples until the next write (`d` −0.15); the third and later writes held 0.3 with no water. No other THIRST change within a minute. The `autodrink.probe` polls ran about 1.26 s apart, not the 0.5 s asked.
- **E** (`Base.Apple` spawned by RCON, `eat.action Base.Apple 1` on `admin`): HUNGER fell 0.3 → 0.14 (dH −0.16, the item's `hungerChange`) at tick 2109, inside minute 57 and with no write, THIRST 0.05 → 0 on the same tick; HUNGER read 0.14 at all 33 samples until the next write (tick 2142), whose pre read 0.14 (`d` −0.16) and post 0.3; the sample after it read 0.3.
- **X** (1040 sampled ticks): HUNGER read 0.3 at every one and every writer delta was 0 (36 writes); the server's copy read `IsRunning` true on 11 ticks, 8 of them with `isPlayerMoving` true, all in the sprint leg; in-`SwipeStatePlayer` false at every tick.
- **U** (`bob`, 5000 calls a run): `update()` cost 22.8, 27.6 and 12.6 µs per call with no hook and 14.8, 17.0 and 11.6 µs with the empty hook registered, differences 8.0, 10.6 and 1.0 µs; STRESS set to 0.5 fell 0.2409, 0.2404 and 0.2396 across each no-hook run and 0 across each hooked run; `benchNoop` 0.13 µs, `benchHookFn` 0.10 µs; the hook was removed after each pair (`hooked` `false`).
- **Z**: no line naming the probe file; `writeErrors` and `sampleErrors` `0`.

(577.2 s wall; `perf.json` 481593 bytes, sha256 `f5021cec…2000bc10`, byte-for-byte identical to the run copy; `MANIFEST.json` sha256 `c86e295a…2415b389`, the staged manifest). **Plan 10b Task P1+P3, the slow minute by step and the cost of no per-tick work**: fixture `two`, `admin` -debug then `bob` release, Nutrition false, DayLength 1, SleepAllowed and SleepNeeded true; every verify row ok; `server_error_count` 0; `admin` not parked; the appended bench file's sha256 `c20f645d…0f8d` equal on the three deployed copies (`meta.deployed_bench_sha256`).

How to read it. Every reading except phase C's is cost, not behaviour (`meta.bench_note`; phase C reads behaviour), and every per-run figure is a 1 ms `getTimestampMs` total divided by a count (`meta.timer_note`). `phases.<A|B|D>.prof.text` is `step=ms/runs/calls` per step of `NutritionRevamp.server.minute`'s ORDER plus `__run` (the pipeline) and `__work` (`P.work`); `prof.parsed` divides them. `calls` counts `NR.call` (with `NR.num`, `NR.obj` and `NR.flag`, which reach it by table lookup), never a direct colon call (`meta.call_counter_note`). The step wrap ran at the first `profStart`, not at `OnServerStarted` (the first `deviations` line).
- **A** (240 runs over 120 game minutes, both players; `phases.A.actions` the minute each action fired at): `__run` 374 ms, `nutrients` 192, `metabolism` 80, `effects` 29, `strength` 17, `weight` 17, `reconcile` 14, `kinetics` 14, `bus` 2, `fast` 1; `__run` calls 18960. Both apples were queued with `validStart` true; the walk read `clientMoving` true on its way back; `admin` read asleep at minute 95. `minstats_*.failures` 0 throughout.
- **B** (`settimespeed 30`, 60 s wall, 606 runs): `__run` 823 ms, `nutrients` 395, `metabolism` 165, `effects` 75, `kinetics` 65.
- **D**: `bench.minute` 430, 389 and 333 µs per call over three runs of 1000; `bench.handler` 20, 22 and 25 µs; `bench_fast` 3.69 µs over 100 000. **D2** (`phases.D.prof`, 1004 runs at an unchanged world age: the 1000 `bench.minute` calls of `phases.D.minute_prof` plus four drain runs; a step under about 4 ms total (`bus`, `fast`, `reconcile`, `kinetics`, `effects`) is at the 1 ms resolution): `__run` 362 ms, `nutrients` 145 with 1036 calls (it returns at a zero interval), `metabolism` 136.
- **C2** (`verdicts.C2.observed`): the burst ran 118 player-runs in 59 events for 145 ms, max 6 ms in one event; the round-robin with `rrM` 5 ran 61 player-runs in 61 events for 105 ms (two players, so each ran every second minute).
- **C** (a behaviour reading, not a cost one; Overlay: `overlay_mode` 2, `overlay_registered` false): the tick ring shows PANIC 0.5 → about 0.32 → about 0.14 → 0 within three ticks of each write at DayLength 1, and a fall of about 0.030 a tick under `time.multiplier` 0.1674 (`phases.C.C4.t.rows`); TEMPERATURE's pre-write reads climb from 36.33 to about 37.48 over the first writes at DayLength 1 (`phases.C.C1.w.rows`, the fourth field) and sit about 37.46 at the slowed clock. The live `sandbox.set DayLength 4` read back 4 but left the clock at 6.47 ticks a game minute (`phases.C.tpm4_daylength`), so C4 ran on the multiplier (`phases.C.c4_route`).
- **Z**: no mod error line; the server's life lines are the takeover's register, removal and re-register around phase C.

**`x232-20261007-121312/panic.json`** and **`x232-20261007-121312/MANIFEST.json`** — produced by `testing/experiments/x232_panic.py`, written before the boot and committed at `12d941f` with the profile `testing/profiles/x23-panic.toml` before the run, unedited since, on HEAD `12d941f` (harness Lua `fadf698`, clean; harness Python `aaa51f4`; `mod/` not booted)
(433.5 s wall; `panic.json` 639482 bytes, sha256 `12c07b99…46bff6d`, byte-for-byte identical to the run copy; `MANIFEST.json` sha256 `c86e295a…2415b389`, the staged manifest). **Plan 10b addendum Task P3b, the PANIC sawtooth at a realistic target**: fixture `two`, `admin` -debug then `bob` release, Nutrition false, DayLength 1, SleepAllowed and SleepNeeded true; every verify row ok; `admin` not parked; the bench file's sha256 `c20f645d…0f8d` equal on the three deployed copies (`meta.deployed_bench_sha256`) and to `testing/spikes/instruments/x231_NR_Server_Bench.lua`; `server_error_count` 516, every one the hold's TEMPERATURE write (below).

How to read it. A behaviour reading, Overlay throughout (`phases.C.overlay_mode` 2, `phases.C.overlay_registered` false). The hold wrote PANIC on both players once a game minute; `tempTarget` was set to nil (`phases.C.setup.temp`), so the TEMPERATURE write raised inside its `pcall` at every write (172 `pcall> Exception thrown` blocks in the run's server console, each a `NullPointerException` unboxing a float at `sset(NR_Server_Bench.lua:217)` from `minuteHold(NR_Server_Bench.lua:251)`, two players times 86 writes; three error lines each, so 516) and TEMPERATURE was not held (the write ring's post-write temperature equals its pre-write one). `phases.C.<leg>.w.rows` is tick, minute, pre-write PANIC, pre-write TEMPERATURE, post-write PANIC, post-write TEMPERATURE, wall ms; `phases.C.<leg>.t.rows` is tick, minute, PANIC, TEMPERATURE read on `OnTick`. `phases.C.<leg>.stats` is the driver's summary, its gap the tick-ring samples after the previous write's tick up to this write's tick (the x231 summary's defect fixed); a gap's last tick-ring sample sits one tick's fall above the pre-write read, because the write ring reads after that tick's decay and the next `OnTick` reads the written value unchanged, so the amplitude (`amp_*`, post-write minus the next pre-write) is the fall per tick times the gap's ticks.
- **C1** (target 10, DayLength 1, 21 gaps of 6 or 7 ticks): the fall per tick 0.17916 over 111 falls (0.17782 to 0.18103), its coefficient of variation 0.0030 against 0.0273 for the fall over the value; the amplitude 1.0713 to 1.2556 (mean 1.1260), the floor before each write 8.7444 to 8.9287.
- **C3** (target 6.5, DayLength 1, 20 gaps): the fall per tick 0.17898; the floor 5.2459 to 5.4297; every gap had samples under 6 (3 or 4 of its 6 or 7), 66 of 126 samples.
- **C2** (target 10, `time.multiplier` 0.1674, 20 gaps of 37 or 38 ticks): the fall per tick 0.030061 over 728 falls (0.029318 to 0.030560); the amplitude 1.1079 to 1.1440 (mean 1.1243), the floor 8.8560 to 8.8921.
- **C3s** (target 6.5, the same clock, 21 gaps): the fall per tick 0.030086; the floor 5.3561 to 5.3883; every gap had samples under 6 (20 or 21 of its 37 or 38), 429 of 786.
- **Moodle**: not read on either side (no harness command reads the PANIC moodle); `inferred_*` fields count the stat against the thresholds 6, 30, 65 and 80 (#2369) and are inference.
- **Z**: no line matched the driver's mod-error pattern, but see the 516 errors above; the server's life lines are the takeover's register, removal and re-register around phase C.
